shaderSource(Shader1, #version 300 es
// Quads built on the CPU (previews, overlays): a rectangle, an atlas cell and a color each.
// Quads are in "a_rect" units; a pixel is round((p + u_offset) * u_scale + u_half).
// World chunks pass the camera there, screen-space quads pass (0, 1, 0).
// A negative layer hides the quad (an animation frame without a sprite).
layout(location=0) in vec2 a_corner;
layout(location=1) in vec4 a_rect;
layout(location=2) in vec2 a_cell;
layout(location=3) in vec4 a_color;
uniform vec2 u_screen;
uniform vec2 u_offset;
uniform float u_scale;
uniform vec2 u_half;
uniform float u_alpha;
uniform float u_perRow;
uniform float u_uvCell;
// Depth of the quads (0: under everything; the play view's player sits among the chunk quads).
uniform float u_depth;
out vec3 v_uv;
out vec4 v_color;
void main() {
  if (a_cell.x < 0.0) {
    gl_Position = vec4(2.0, 2.0, 2.0, 1.0);
    v_uv = vec3(0.0);
    v_color = vec4(0.0);
    return;
  }
  vec2 world = a_rect.xy + a_corner * a_rect.zw;
  vec2 pixel = floor((world + u_offset) * u_scale + u_half + 0.5);
  gl_Position = vec4(pixel.x / u_screen.x * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - pixel.y / u_screen.y * 2.0, u_depth, 1.0);
  float layer = a_cell.x;
  float column = mod(a_cell.y, u_perRow);
  float row = floor(a_cell.y / u_perRow);
  v_uv = vec3((vec2(column, row) + a_corner) * u_uvCell, layer);
  v_color = vec4(a_color.rgb, a_color.a * u_alpha);
}
)
shaderSource(Shader2, #version 300 es
// Atlas texel tinted by the quad color; transparent texels are discarded.
precision mediump float;
precision mediump sampler2DArray;
uniform sampler2DArray u_tex;
in vec3 v_uv;
in vec4 v_color;
out vec4 outColor;
void main() {
  vec4 texel = texture(u_tex, v_uv);
  if (texel.a == 0.0) discard;
  outColor = texel * v_color;
}
)
shaderSource(Shader3, #version 300 es
// Chunk quads (QUAD_BYTES each, see geo.ts): the sprite id is looked up in
// the cell index (composed outfit pieces in their own index). The depth is
// the drawing rank of the quad's tile: chunks are drawn whole, so a quad of
// a later chunk must not cover one of an earlier chunk's tile drawn after
// it (a sprite reaching up into the chunk above, see map_geo.go). Top items
// (tile y + TILE_TOP) rank half a tile later: the play view draws creatures
// between a tile's items and its top items (see PLAY.playerRank in constants.ts).
layout(location=0) in vec2 a_corner;
layout(location=1) in vec2 a_pos;
layout(location=2) in uint a_sprite;
layout(location=3) in vec4 a_color;
layout(location=4) in vec2 a_tile;
uniform vec2 u_screen;
uniform vec2 u_offset;
// The chunk's corner tile from the tile the ranks start at.
uniform vec2 u_tile;
uniform float u_spriteSize;
uniform float u_scale;
uniform vec2 u_half;
uniform float u_alpha;
uniform uint u_perRow;
uniform uint u_perLayer;
uniform float u_uvCell;
uniform highp usampler2D u_spriteCells;
uniform highp usampler2D u_composedCells;
out vec3 v_uv;
out vec4 v_color;
const uint MISSING = 0xFFFFFFFFu;
// Ranks from -DEPTH_RANGE to DEPTH_RANGE fit the depth range.
const float DEPTH_RANGE = 524288.0;
const float TILE_TOP = 32768.0;
uint lookup(highp usampler2D cells, uint id) {
  ivec2 size = textureSize(cells, 0);
  uint width = uint(size.x);
  if (id >= width * uint(size.y)) return MISSING;
  return texelFetch(cells, ivec2(int(id % width), int(id / width)), 0).r;
}
void main() {
  uint cell = MISSING;
  vec2 size = vec2(u_spriteSize);
  if (a_sprite != MISSING) {
    if ((a_sprite & 0x80000000u) != 0u) cell = lookup(u_composedCells, a_sprite & 0x7FFFFFFFu);
    else if ((a_sprite & 0x40000000u) != 0u) {
      // A colored rectangle: the white cell, its size in the value.
      cell = 0u;
      size = vec2(float((a_sprite >> 15) & 0x7FFFu), float(a_sprite & 0x7FFFu));
    } else cell = lookup(u_spriteCells, a_sprite);
  }
  if (cell == MISSING) {
    gl_Position = vec4(2.0, 2.0, 2.0, 1.0);
    v_uv = vec3(0.0);
    v_color = vec4(0.0);
    return;
  }
  vec2 world = a_pos + a_corner * size;
  vec2 pixel = floor((world + u_offset) * u_scale + u_half + 0.5);
  // Diagonals from the top-left, each from its bottom-left end, as
  // map_geo.go orders tiles; exact for tiles less than 8 apart, the only
  // ones whose sprites overlap.
  vec2 tile = a_tile;
  float top = 0.0;
  if (tile.y >= TILE_TOP) {
    tile.y -= TILE_TOP;
    top = 0.5;
  }
  vec2 ranked = tile + u_tile;
  float rank = 8.0 * (ranked.x + ranked.y) + ranked.x + top;
  gl_Position = vec4(pixel.x / u_screen.x * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - pixel.y / u_screen.y * 2.0, clamp(rank / DEPTH_RANGE, -1.0, 1.0), 1.0);
  uint cellInLayer = cell % u_perLayer;
  v_uv = vec3((vec2(float(cellInLayer % u_perRow), float(cellInLayer / u_perRow)) + a_corner) * u_uvCell, float(cell / u_perLayer));
  v_color = vec4(a_color.rgb, a_color.a * u_alpha);
}
)
shaderSource(Shader4, #version 300 es
// Atlas texel tinted by the quad color; transparent texels are discarded.
precision mediump float;
precision mediump sampler2DArray;
uniform sampler2DArray u_tex;
in vec3 v_uv;
in vec4 v_color;
out vec4 outColor;
void main() {
  vec4 texel = texture(u_tex, v_uv);
  if (texel.a == 0.0) discard;
  outColor = texel * v_color;
}
)
shaderSource(Shader5, #version 300 es
// The light map stretched over its tiles: a rectangle in screen pixels.
layout(location=0) in vec2 a_corner;
uniform vec2 u_screen;
uniform vec4 u_rect;
out vec2 v_uv;
void main() {
  vec2 pixel = u_rect.xy + a_corner * u_rect.zw;
  gl_Position = vec4(pixel.x / u_screen.x * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - pixel.y / u_screen.y * 2.0, 0.0, 1.0);
  v_uv = a_corner;
}
)
shaderSource(Shader6, #version 300 es
// One texel per tile, filtered linearly: the light fades from tile to tile.
precision mediump float;
uniform sampler2D u_light;
in vec2 v_uv;
out vec4 outColor;
void main() {
  outColor = vec4(texture(u_light, v_uv).rgb, 1.0);
}
)
texParameteri(TEXTURE_2D, TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, LINEAR)
texParameteri(TEXTURE_2D, TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, LINEAR)
texParameteri(TEXTURE_2D, TEXTURE_WRAP_S, CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
texParameteri(TEXTURE_2D, TEXTURE_WRAP_T, CLAMP_TO_EDGE)
vertexAttribDivisor(1, 1)
vertexAttribDivisor(2, 1)
vertexAttribDivisor(3, 1)
vertexAttribPointer(0, 2, FLOAT, false, 0, 0)
vertexAttribPointer(0, 2, FLOAT, false, 0, 0)
vertexAttribPointer(1, 4, FLOAT, false, 40, 0)
vertexAttribPointer(2, 2, FLOAT, false, 40, 16)
vertexAttribPointer(3, 4, FLOAT, false, 40, 24)

createTexture() -> Texture4
bindTexture(TEXTURE_2D_ARRAY, Texture4)
texStorage3D(TEXTURE_2D_ARRAY, 1, RGBA8, 1024, 1024, 4)
texParameteri(TEXTURE_2D_ARRAY, TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, NEAREST)
texParameteri(TEXTURE_2D_ARRAY, TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, NEAREST)
